# Agents · I · part 2

967 entries, implementer to instruction-advisor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **implementer**: Executes exactly one task from an approved spec or plan, test-first, in an isolated worktree. Dispatch one implementer per task; give it the spec path and the single task it owns. ([craftycorvid/powers](https://github.com/craftycorvid/powers/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implementation agent for plan execution. Receives a single task with full spec text — no plan file reading required. Writes code following TDD, self-reviews before reporting, commits per task, and flags blockers immediately rather than guessing. ([danieliser/strategic-planner](https://github.com/danieliser/strategic-planner/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Use to implement an approved design in code with tests (Phase 3 of the SDLC pipeline). Writes the code and tests, runs the build/tests, and produces 03-implementation.md (files changed, decisions, test results). &lt;example&gt; Context: Design approved at Gate 2; orchestrator starts Phase 3. user: "(approved 02-design.md attached)" assistant: "I'll use the implementer agent to build it and write 03-implementation.md." &lt;commentary&gt;Implementation phase — write code + tests against the approved design.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([danniel-isiah-libor/danniel-claude-plugins](https://github.com/danniel-isiah-libor/danniel-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-sdlc-team/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Desarrollador senior que implementa features siguiendo planes de implementación. Usa este agente para ejecutar un plan creado por el planner o para implementar cambios de código. ([devlitus/travel-web](https://github.com/devlitus/travel-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **Implementer**: Implementa features siguiendo planes de implementación detallados. Escribe código, crea archivos y ejecuta comandos. ([devlitus/travel-web](https://github.com/devlitus/travel-web/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Make one plan task's failing tests pass — smallest correct change, repo conventions, nothing beyond the task. ([dngioidev/forge](https://github.com/dngioidev/forge/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **Implementer**: Use when writing production code, implementing features, adding API endpoints, creating data models, or writing unit and integration tests. Follows SDLC Phase 4 with test-driven implementation. ([Dongbumlee/sdlc-harness](https://github.com/Dongbumlee/sdlc-harness/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Implementation delegate that executes exactly one task node per dispatch for the example project and returns evidence against that node's acceptance criteria. ([DuBois-Company/agent-operations-template](https://github.com/DuBois-Company/agent-operations-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a board task or an OpenSpec change group in a worktree — production code and its unit tests against an already-fixed contract (specs, scenario tests, task checklist). ([EduardoIllanes/ratchet](https://github.com/EduardoIllanes/ratchet/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implementer of the /feature-dev pipeline. Receives ONE work package from the feature plan and implements it test-driven, staying within the WP's file boundaries. Dispatched in parallel by the orchestrator for file-disjoint WPs of the same wave. ([Elverle/agentic-feature-factory](https://github.com/Elverle/agentic-feature-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Junior engineer. Call for a bounded code, test, or documentation edit that has a check the child can run alone, typically after a senior wrote the interface, docstrings, and test list with the engineer skill. Fills bodies and tests inside the allowed paths, runs the check, returns the fixed report. Not for shared-contract changes, GPU jobs, git operations, or anything without a runnable check. ([emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config](https://github.com/emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config/blob/HEAD/.agent-rules/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: TDD implementation worker for /ship pipeline phases — writes the code volume on Opus 4.8 while the orchestrator (Opus 4.8) plans, orchestrates and merge-gates. Receives a scoped task from an approved plan; tests first, then implementation, then green. ([erhanuenlue/tarifhub](https://github.com/erhanuenlue/tarifhub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Use this agent to implement a single task from an implementation plan. Dispatch with the full task spec text, context about where it fits, and the working directory. Invoke via `bin/sotp capability exec` — never directly through the Agent tool: direct Agent-tool invocation bypasses provider / model resolution, while `bin/sotp capability exec` is the canonical route that internally resolves them from `.harness/config/agent-profiles.json`. - **implementer**: The squad's implementer. Implements one subproject's slice of a feature from its tasks.md, following the stack and idioms the project profile declares. Activate (one instance per subproject, in parallel) after the planner produces tasks.md. Each instance owns exactly one subproject — it executes only the tasks whose ID carries that subproject's prefix, writes code AND its tests, runs the subproject's analyze/lint + test suite until green, then commits and opens a PR to develop. Stack-agnostic: reads the project profile at runtime. ([gustavobaptista/specops](https://github.com/gustavobaptista/specops/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Full-stack code implementer. Use for writing features, fixing bugs, creating components, modifying code. Handles TypeScript, Python, Swift, and any stack found in the project. - **INDEX**: These files are repository-defined pipeline agents. - **index**: Antiphon listens to agent sessions through small adapters that talk to the local `antiphond` daemon. ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/docs/agents/index.md) · ★1)
